1. TS-OT-TEST-003 — Capture-replay compromise of a Mitsubishi MELSEC WS Ethernet interface
An attacker with control of an engineering workstation and the ability to observe OT traffic. Through the established path, the attacker reaches the network path to the MELSEC module.
CVE-2015-6464 affects MELSEC WS0-GETH00200. If the deployed version falls within this affected range, the attacker can exploit the incorrect-authorization vulnerability.
The attacker replays captured authentication data to attempt unauthorized login.
CVE-2023-6374 affects MELSEC WS0-GETH00200. If the deployed version falls within this affected range, the attacker can exploit the validated vulnerability.
As a result, the availability and integrity of protection, control, monitoring, or communication functions may be impaired.
